public class TC extends java.lang.Object {

        // @method <init>()V

    }

    }

        // @method argPos(I)I

        // recovered from bytecode; presentation is not claimed to compile
        foo(arg0 > 0 ? 1 : 2);
        return arg0;
    }

    }

    static java.lang.String condCond(boolean arg0, boolean arg1) {

        // @method condCond(ZZ)Ljava/lang/String;

        // @bytecode 0 1 4 5 8 10 13 14 17 18 21 23 26 28

    }

    }
